Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in macroevolution theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 686 and the invariant I(A,B) = 226 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the macroevolution space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 686 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 226. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
